# 子 Agent · 你要为一个「ShopStack 电商中台」项目开发 **Vue3 用户端前台**（面向消费者的 Web 商城），必须能真实构建通过。

> 第 1 / 2 卷

## 👤 用户 · 2026-10-20 11:49:56

你要为一个「ShopStack 电商中台」项目开发 **Vue3 用户端前台**（面向消费者的 Web 商城），必须能真实构建通过。

## 项目位置与你的文件所有权
- 项目根目录：`/<WORKSPACE>/shopstack`
- **你只拥有并创建 `shopstack/apps/web-vue/` 目录下的文件。** 不要修改项目其他任何目录（后端、proto、design-tokens、其他 app 目录都归别人所有）。

## 必读的现有文件（不要修改，只读）
1. `shopstack/design-tokens/tokens.json` —— 唯一设计令牌数据源；已生成产物在 `shopstack/design-tokens/generated/`：
   - `tokens.css`（CSS 变量，变量名形如 `--ss-color-brand-600`、`--ss-radius-lg`、`--ss-shadow-md`）→ **你的项目直接 import 这个文件**
   - `tokens.ts`（类型化常量）
2. `shopstack/proto/shopstack/*/v1/*.proto` —— **接口契约真相源**。你必须逐个读取 `user/v1/user.proto`、`product/v1/product.proto`、`cart/v1/cart.proto`、`order/v1/order.proto`、`payment/v1/payment.proto`、`marketing/v1/marketing.proto`、`logistics/v1/logistics.proto`、`notification/v1/notification.proto`，严格按其中的 **字段名和类型** 写 TS 类型定义。不要臆造字段名。
3. `shopstack/deploy/mysql/init/02-seed.sql` —— 了解种子数据长什么样（商品分类、商品、优惠券等），用于你的 Mock 兜底数据与文案。

## 技术栈（硬性要求）
- Vite 5+ + Vue 3（`<script setup lang="ts">`）+ TypeScript + Pinia + Vue Router 4 + Axios
- **不要引入重型 UI 组件库**（不要 Element Plus / Ant Design Vue）。用设计令牌 + 手写轻量组件实现现代简洁 SaaS 风（蓝白、卡片、柔和阴影、圆角）。
- 状态管理用 Pinia，接口层统一封装。

## 后端契约（后端由别人并行实现，你按此对接）
- 网关地址：`http://localhost:8080`，API 前缀 `/api/v1`。
- **统一响应信封**（务必按此解析）：
  ```json
  { "code": 0, "message": "ok", "data": { ... }, "trace_id": "..." }
  ```
  `code === 0` 为成功，否则是业务错误，`message` 可直接展示给用户。
- 鉴权：登录后拿到 `data.access_token` / `data.refresh_token`，请求头带 `Authorization: Bearer <access_token>`。token 存 localStorage。401 时用 refresh_token 静默刷新并重放原请求（只重放一次）。
- **金额单位是「分」（整数）**，展示时格式化为 `¥1,299.00`；请写一个 `formatYuan(cents)` 工具函数。
- 时间字段：proto 里用 `google.protobuf.Timestamp` 的字段，JSON 序列化为 RFC3339 字符串，如 `2026-10-20T03:12:45Z`。
- 枚举字段在 JSON 里是**字符串**（如 `"ORDER_STATUS_PAID"`、`"PRODUCT_STATUS_ON_SHELF"`、`"USER_COUPON_STATUS_UNUSED"`），请写枚举映射工具把它们转成中文文案。
- 完整接口清单（方法 + 路径）见下方附录。请在 `src/api/` 下按业务域拆分 api 模块（auth.ts、product.ts、cart.ts、order.ts、payment.ts、marketing.ts、logistics.ts、user.ts、notification.ts）。

## 必须实现的功能与页面
**路由结构**
- `/` 首页
- `/category/:id?` 分类页（含左侧分类导航 + 右侧商品网格 + 排序/筛选）
- `/product/:id` 商品详情
- `/cart` 购物车
- `/checkout` 结算确认
- `/order` 订单列表（tab：全部/待付款/待发货/待收货/退款）
- `/order/:id` 订单详情
- `/user` 个人中心
- `/user/address` 地址管理
- `/user/favorites` 我的收藏
- `/user/coupons` 我的优惠券
- `/login` 登录（账号密码 + 微信登录入口 + 注册）
- `/search?q=` 搜索结果页

**页面要求（都要是真实可交互的实现，不是静态稿）**
1. **首页**：顶部搜索栏 + 分类快捷入口（横向滚动）+ 轮播图（自动播放/指示器）+ 限时秒杀/促销模块 + 商品推荐网格（卡片：图片、标题、价格、销量、加入购物车按钮）。轮播可用轻量自实现或 `swiper` 库；建议自实现避免依赖风险。
2. **分类页**：左侧一级分类（支持二级），右侧该分类商品列表；支持按 销量/价格/最新 排序、价格区间筛选、仅看有货。
3. **商品详情**：轮播图、标题/副标题、价格（原价划线价 + 现价）、销量、SKU 规格选择（颜色/容量等，用 `skus[].specs` 渲染可选项，选择后联动价格/库存/图片）、商品参数（attributes）、用户评价列表 + 评分分布、相关推荐。底部操作栏：**加入购物车** / **立即购买**（立即购买跳转结算）。收藏按钮（调用 ToggleFavorite）。
4. **购物车**：分组列表（可按店铺概念分组，这里按商品分类分组即可），每项有复选框、数量步进器（`UpdateCartItem`）、删除、失效商品置灰标记；底部固定结算栏：全选、已选数量、合计金额、结算按钮。库存不足要明确提示。
5. **结算页**：收货地址选择（无地址时引导新增）、商品清单、优惠券选择（`GET /api/v1/users/coupons` 可用券，配合 `POST /api/v1/marketing/preview` 实时试算）、金额明细（商品总额/优惠/运费/应付）、提交订单（`POST /api/v1/orders`）。提交后跳支付页。
6. **支付页**：选择支付方式（微信/支付宝/余额），调用 `POST /api/v1/payments` 创建支付单，展示支付参数，模拟支付流程（轮询 `GET /api/v1/payments/query`），支付结果页。
7. **订单列表 / 详情**：按状态分 tab，订单卡片（订单号、时间、状态、商品缩略图、金额），详情页有状态时间线（用 `GET /api/v1/orders/{id}/logs`）、物流查询（`GET /api/v1/logistics/order/{order_id}`）、可执行操作：取消订单、确认收货、申请退款、评价。
8. **个人中心**：用户信息卡（头像/昵称/积分/余额）、订单状态入口（带角标数量，用 `GET /api/v1/orders/count`）、功能宫格（地址/收藏/优惠券/通知/退出登录）。
9. **登录页**：账号密码登录/注册、微信登录（`POST /api/v1/auth/wechat`，演示环境后端返回 mock 用户也能跑通）、表单校验、加载态、错误提示。

## 工程要求
- `src/api/client.ts`：Axios 实例 + baseURL + 请求/响应拦截器（注入 token、解包信封、401 刷新、错误 toast 提示、trace_id 透传）。
- `src/stores/`：auth（token/用户信息/登录登出/刷新）、cart（购物车条目 + 数量角标）、user、app（全局提示）。
- `src/components/`：至少实现 `ProductCard`、`PriceText`、`QuantityStepper`、`AppHeader`、`AppFooter`、`EmptyState`、`LoadingState`、`Skeleton`、`Pagination`、`AddressPicker`（可复用组件）。
- `src/utils/`：`format.ts`（金额/时间/数量格式化）、`enums.ts`（枚举中文映射）、`auth.ts`（token 存取）。
- **网络异常与后端未启动时要有兜底**：API 调用失败时，接口层返回内置 Mock 数据（Mock 数据放在 `src/mock/` 下，字段结构必须与 proto 完全一致），并在页面上用轻量提示条说明当前为演示数据模式。这样在后端未就绪时前端依然能完整展示。
- **响应式**：≥1280px 多栏；768–1279px 收紧；<768px 移动端布局（栅格降列、底部固定操作栏、抽屉式分类导航）。
- TypeScript strict 模式，`npm run build` 必须零错误通过。
- 代码注释用中文，风格与项目一致（简洁、直接）。

## 交付验收标准
1. `cd shopstack/apps/web-vue && npm install && npm run build` **成功**，产出 `dist/`。
2. `npm run dev` 能启动且首页正常渲染（Mock 兜底也要能渲染）。
3. 上述所有页面路由可访问，无运行时报错。
4. 没有任何 `TODO` / `FIXME` / 假按钮（点了没反应的按钮要接上真实逻辑或禁用并给出提示）。
5. 代码结构清晰，组件职责单一。

## 执行方式
1. 先读 `design-tokens/generated/tokens.css` 和相关 proto 文件，建立字段认知。
2. 用 `npm create vite@latest` 的等价命令（`npm create vite@latest web-vue -- --template vue-ts`）在 `shopstack/apps/` 下创建项目，**注意它会在当前目录建子目录**，请确保最终路径是 `shopstack/apps/web-vue`。
3. 自行安装依赖：axios、pinia、vue-router。若某个包安装失败，改用原生 fetch 实现同名工具函数，不要卡住。
4. 分批实现，最后务必跑通 build。

## 最终回复格式
简短汇报（≤200 字）：实现了哪些页面、build 是否通过、mock 兜底策略、以及任何你做的与要求的偏离。不要粘贴大段代码。

```
     3→package shopstack.user.v1;

     6→
     7→import "google/api/annotations.proto";
     8→import "google/protobuf/timestamp.proto";
     9→import "shopstack/common/v1/common.proto";
    10→
    11→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→// 实体
    13→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→message User {
    16→  int64 id = 1;
    17→  string username = 2;
    18→  string nickname = 3;
    19→  string avatar = 4;
    20→  string phone = 5;
    21→  string email = 6;
    22→  shopstack.common.v1.Gender gender = 7;
    23→  string birthday = 8;
    24→  int32 points = 9;   // 积分
    25→  int64 balance_cents = 10; // 余额（分）
    26→  shopstack.common.v1.UserStatus status = 11;
    27→  string wechat_openid = 12;
    28→  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 13;
    29→  google.protobuf.Timestamp last_login_at = 14;
    30→}
    31→
    32→message Admin {
    33→  int64 id = 1;
    34→  string username = 2;
    35→  string real_name = 3;
    36→  string avatar = 4;
    37→  string email = 5;
    38→  string phone = 6;
    39→  repeated int64 role_ids = 7;
    40→  repeated string role_names = 8;
    41→  int32 status = 9;
    42→  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 10;
    43→  google.protobuf.Timestamp last_login_at = 11;
    44→}
    45→
    46→message Role {
    47→  int64 id = 1;
    48→  string name = 2;
    49→  string code = 3;
    50→  string description = 4;
    51→  repeated int64 permission_ids = 5;
    52→  repeated string permission_codes = 6;
    53→  int32 user_count = 7;
    54→  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 8;
    55→}
    56→
    57→message Permission {
    58→  int64 id = 1;
    59→  string name = 2;

    62→  string type = 5;   // menu | action
    63→  string parent_code = 6;
    64→  int32 sort = 7;
    65→}
    66→
    67→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    68→// 认证

    81→message RegisterRequest {

    85→  string nickname = 4;
    86→  string sms_code = 5;

    89→message LoginRequest {

    91→  string password = 2;
    92→}

    94→message WeChatLoginRequest {

    96→  string nickname = 2;
    97→  string avatar = 3;
    98→}
    99→
   100→message RefreshTokenRequest {
   101→  string refresh_token = 1;
   102→}
   103→
   104→message ChangePasswordRequest {

   247→

   286→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

   289→
   290→service UserService {
   291→  rpc Register(RegisterRequest) returns (AuthResponse) {
   292→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/auth/register" body: "*" };
   293→  }
   294→  rpc Login(LoginRequest) returns (AuthResponse) {
   295→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/auth/login" body: "*" };
   296→  }
   297→  rpc LoginWithWeChat(WeChatLoginRequest) returns (AuthResponse) {
   298→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/auth/wechat" body: "*" };
   299→  }
   300→  rpc RefreshToken(RefreshTokenRequest) returns (AuthResponse) {
   301→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/auth/refresh" body: "*" };
   302→  }
   303→  rpc Logout(shopstack.common.v1.Empty) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   304→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/auth/logout" body: "*" };
   305→  }
   306→  rpc GetProfile(GetProfileRequest) returns (User) {
   307→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/users/me" };
   308→  }
   309→  rpc UpdateProfile(UpdateProfileRequest) returns (User) {
   310→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/users/me" body: "*" };
   311→  }
   312→  rpc ChangePassword(ChangePasswordRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   313→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/users/password" body: "*" };
   314→  }
   315→  rpc ListAddresses(ListAddressesRequest) returns (ListAddressesResponse) {
   316→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/users/addresses" };
   317→  }
   318→  rpc SaveAddress(SaveAddressRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.Address) {
   319→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/users/addresses" body: "*" };
   320→  }
   321→  rpc DeleteAddress(DeleteAddressRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   322→    option (google.api.http) = { delete: "/api/v1/users/addresses/{id}" };
   323→  }
   324→  rpc SetDefaultAddress(SetDefaultAddressRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   325→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/users/addresses/{id}/default" body: "*" };
   326→  }
   327→  rpc ListFavorites(ListFavoritesRequest) returns (ListFavoritesResponse) {
   328→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/users/favorites" };
   329→  }
   330→  rpc ToggleFavorite(ToggleFavoriteRequest) returns (ToggleFavoriteResponse) {
   331→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/users/favorites/toggle" body: "*" };
   332→  }
   333→}
   334→
   335→service AdminService {
   336→  rpc Login(AdminLoginRequest) returns (AuthResponse) {
   337→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/admin/auth/login" body: "*" };
   338→  }
   339→  rpc GetProfile(GetProfileRequest) returns (Admin) {
   340→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/admin/me" };
   341→  }
   342→  rpc UpdateProfile(AdminUpdateProfileRequest) returns (Admin) {
   343→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/admin/me" body: "*" };
   344→  }
   345→  rpc ListUsers(AdminListUsersRequest) returns (AdminListUsersResponse) {
   346→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/admin/users" };
   347→  }
   348→  rpc UpdateUserStatus(AdminUpdateUserStatusRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   349→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/admin/users/{user_id}/status" body: "*" };
   350→  }
   351→  rpc ListRoles(AdminListRolesRequest) returns (AdminListRolesResponse) {
   352→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/admin/roles" };
   353→  }
   354→  rpc SaveRole(SaveRoleRequest) returns (Role) {
   355→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/admin/roles" body: "*" };
   356→  }
   357→  rpc DeleteRole(DeleteRoleRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   358→    option (google.api.http) = { delete: "/api/v1/admin/roles/{id}" };
   359→  }
   360→  rpc ListPermissions(AdminListPermissionsRequest) returns (AdminListPermissionsResponse) {
   361→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/admin/permissions" };
   362→  }
   363→  rpc DashboardOverview(AdminDashboardOverviewRequest) returns (AdminDashboardOverviewResponse) {
   364→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/admin/dashboard/overview" };
   365→  }
   366→}
```

```
1→syntax = "proto3";
     2→
     3→package shopstack.product.v1;
     4→
     5→option go_package = "github.com/shopstack/platform/gen/go/product/v1;productv1";
     6→
     7→import "google/api/annotations.proto";
     8→import "google/protobuf/timestamp.proto";
     9→import "shopstack/common/v1/common.proto";
    10→
    11→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→// 实体
    13→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→message Category {
    16→  int64 id = 1;
    17→  string name = 2;
    18→  int64 parent_id = 3;
    19→  string icon = 4;
    20→  string description = 5;
    21→  int32 sort = 6;
    22→  bool visible = 7;
    23→  int32 product_count = 8;
    24→  repeated Category children = 9;
    25→}
    26→
    27→message Product {
    28→  int64 id = 1;
    29→  string title = 2;
    30→  string subtitle = 3;
    31→  string cover = 4;
    32→  int64 category_id = 5;
    33→  string category_name = 6;
    34→  int64 min_price_cents = 7;
    35→  int64 max_price_cents = 8;
    36→  int64 sales_count = 9;
    37→  int32 stock_total = 10;
    38→  shopstack.common.v1.ProductStatus status = 11;
    39→  string description = 12;
    40→  repeated string tags = 13;
    41→  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 14;
    42→  google.protobuf.Timestamp updated_at = 15;
    43→}
    44→
    45→message ProductSku {
    46→  int64 id = 1;
    47→  int64 product_id = 2;
    48→  string sku_code = 3;
    49→  string spec_text = 4;         // 规格文案，如 "红色 / XL"
    50→  map<string, string> specs = 5; // 属性键值
    51→  int64 price_cents = 6;
    52→  int64 original_price_cents = 7;
    53→  int32 stock = 8;
    54→  int32 locked_stock = 9;
    55→  string image = 10;
    56→  int32 sort = 11;
    57→  int32 sales_count = 12;
    58→  bool enabled = 13;
    59→  int64 weight_grams = 14;
    60→}
    61→
    62→message ProductDetail {
    63→  Product product = 1;
    64→  repeated ProductSku skus = 2;
    65→  repeated shopstack.common.v1.ImageItem images = 3;
    66→  repeated ProductAttribute attributes = 4;
    67→  repeated ReviewSummary reviews = 5;
    68→  int32 review_count = 6;
    69→  double review_rating = 7;
    70→  repeated Product related = 8;
    71→}
    72→
    73→message ProductAttribute {
    74→  int64 id = 1;
    75→  int64 product_id = 2;
    76→  string name = 3;
    77→  string value = 4;
    78→  int32 sort = 5;
    79→}
    80→
    81→message Banner {
    82→  int64 id = 1;
    83→  string title = 2;
    84→  string image = 3;
    85→  string link = 4;
    86→  int32 sort = 5;
    87→  string position = 6; // home_banner | home_float | category
    88→  bool visible = 7;
    89→  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 8;
    90→}
    91→
    92→message Review {
    93→  int64 id = 1;
    94→  int64 product_id = 2;
    95→  string product_title = 3;
    96→  int64 user_id = 4;
    97→  string user_nickname = 5;
    98→  string user_avatar = 6;
    99→  int32 rating = 7;
   100→  string content = 8;
   101→  repeated string images = 9;
   102→  int32 sku_id = 10;
   103→  string sku_text = 11;
   104→  shopstack.common.v1.ReviewStatus status = 12;
   105→  string admin_reply = 13;
   106→  int32 like_count = 14;
   107→  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 15;
   108→}
   109→
   110→message ReviewSummary {
   111→  int32 total = 1;
   112→  int32 good = 2;      // 4-5 星
   113→  int32 medium = 3;    // 3 星
   114→  int32 bad = 4;       // 1-2 星
   115→  double average = 5;
   116→}
   117→
   118→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→// 请求 / 响应
   120→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→
   122→message ListProductsRequest {
   123→  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 1;
   124→  string keyword = 2;
   125→  int64 category_id = 3;
   126→  int32 status = 4;
   127→  int64 min_price_cents = 5;
   128→  int64 max_price_cents = 6;
   129→  string sort = 7; // default | sales | price_asc | price_desc | newest
   130→  bool only_in_stock = 8;
   131→  repeated string tags = 9;
   132→}
   133→
   134→message ListProductsResponse {
   135→  repeated Product products = 1;
   136→  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
   137→}
   138→
   139→message GetProductRequest {
   140→  int64 id = 1;
   141→}
   142→
   143→message SaveProductRequest {
   144→  int64 id = 1;
   145→  string title = 2;
   146→  string subtitle = 3;
   147→  string cover = 4;
   148→  int64 category_id = 5;
   149→  int64 min_price_cents = 6;
   150→  int64 max_price_cents = 7;
   151→  string description = 8;
   152→  repeated string tags = 9;
   153→  int32 status = 10;
   154→  repeated shopstack.common.v1.ImageItem images = 11;
   155→  repeated ProductAttribute attributes = 12;
   156→  repeated ProductSku skus = 13;
   157→}
   158→
   159→message DeleteProductRequest {
   160→  int64 id = 1;
   161→}
   162→
   163→message SetProductStatusRequest {
   164→  int64 id = 1;
   165→  int32 status = 2;
   166→}
   167→
   168→message SaveSkuRequest {
   169→  int64 id = 1;
   170→  int64 product_id = 2;
   171→  string sku_code = 3;
   172→  string spec_text = 4;
   173→  map<string, string> specs = 5;
   174→  int64 price_cents = 6;
   175→  int64 original_price_cents = 7;
   176→  int32 stock = 8;
   177→  string image = 9;
   178→  int32 sort = 10;
   179→  bool enabled = 11;
   180→  int64 weight_grams = 12;
   181→}
   182→
   183→message ListCategoriesRequest {
   184→  int64 parent_id = 1;
   185→  bool tree = 2;
   186→}
   187→
   188→message ListCategoriesResponse {
   189→  repeated Category categories = 1;
   190→}
   191→
   192→message SaveCategoryRequest {
   193→  int64 id = 1;
   194→  string name = 2;
   195→  int64 parent_id = 3;
   196→  string icon = 4;
   197→  string description = 5;
   198→  int32 sort = 6;
   199→  bool visible = 7;
   200→}
   201→
   202→message DeleteCategoryRequest {
   203→  int64 id = 1;
   204→}
   205→
   206→message ListBannersRequest {
   207→  string position = 1;
   208→}
   209→
   210→message ListBannersResponse {
   211→  repeated Banner banners = 1;
   212→}
   213→
   214→message SaveBannerRequest {
   215→  int64 id = 1;
   216→  string title = 2;
   217→  string image = 3;
   218→  string link = 4;
   219→  int32 sort = 5;
   220→  string position = 6;
   221→  bool visible = 7;
   222→}
   223→
   224→message DeleteBannerRequest {
   225→  int64 id = 1;
   226→}
   227→
   228→message ListReviewsRequest {
   229→  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 1;
   230→  int64 product_id = 2;
   231→  int32 status = 3;
   232→  int64 user_id = 4;
   233→  string keyword = 5;
   234→}
   235→
   236→message ListReviewsResponse {
   237→  repeated Review reviews = 1;
   238→  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
   239→  ReviewSummary summary = 3;
   240→}
   241→
   242→message CreateReviewRequest {
   243→  int64 user_id = 1;
   244→  int64 product_id = 2;
   245→  int64 sku_id = 3;
   246→  int32 rating = 4;
   247→  string content = 5;
   248→  repeated string images = 6;
   249→}
   250→
   251→message SetReviewStatusRequest {
   252→  int64 id = 1;
   253→  int32 status = 2;
   254→  string admin_reply = 3;
   255→}
   256→
   257→message ListProductAttributesRequest {
   258→  int64 product_id = 1;
   259→}
   260→
   261→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   262→// 服务定义
   263→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   264→
   265→service ProductService {
   266→  rpc ListProducts(ListProductsRequest) returns (ListProductsResponse) {
   267→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/products" };
   268→  }
   269→  rpc GetProduct(GetProductRequest) returns (ProductDetail) {
   270→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/products/{id}" };
   271→  }
   272→  rpc CreateProduct(SaveProductRequest) returns (Product) {
   273→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/admin/products" body: "*" };
   274→  }
   275→  rpc UpdateProduct(SaveProductRequest) returns (Product) {
   276→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/admin/products" body: "*" };
   277→  }
   278→  rpc DeleteProduct(DeleteProductRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   279→    option (google.api.http) = { delete: "/api/v1/admin/products/{id}" };
   280→  }
   281→  rpc SetProductStatus(SetProductStatusRequest) returns (Product) {
   282→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/admin/products/{id}/status" body: "*" };
   283→  }
   284→  rpc SaveSku(SaveSkuRequest) returns (ProductSku) {
   285→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/admin/skus" body: "*" };
   286→  }
   287→  rpc ListBanners(ListBannersRequest) returns (ListBannersResponse) {
   288→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/banners" };
   289→  }
   290→  rpc SaveBanner(SaveBannerRequest) returns (Banner) {
   291→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/admin/banners" body: "*" };
   292→  }
   293→  rpc DeleteBanner(DeleteBannerRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   294→    option (google.api.http) = { delete: "/api/v1/admin/banners/{id}" };
   295→  }
   296→}
   297→
   298→service CategoryService {
   299→  rpc ListCategories(ListCategoriesRequest) returns (ListCategoriesResponse) {
   300→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/categories" };
   301→  }
   302→  rpc CreateCategory(SaveCategoryRequest) returns (Category) {
   303→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/admin/categories" body: "*" };
   304→  }
   305→  rpc UpdateCategory(SaveCategoryRequest) returns (Category) {
   306→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/admin/categories" body: "*" };
   307→  }
   308→  rpc DeleteCategory(DeleteCategoryRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   309→    option (google.api.http) = { delete: "/api/v1/admin/categories/{id}" };
   310→  }
   311→}
   312→
   313→service ReviewService {
   314→  rpc ListReviews(ListReviewsRequest) returns (ListReviewsResponse) {
   315→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/reviews" };
   316→  }
   317→  rpc CreateReview(CreateReviewRequest) returns (Review) {
   318→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/reviews" body: "*" };
   319→  }
   320→  rpc SetReviewStatus(SetReviewStatusRequest) returns (Review) {
   321→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/admin/reviews/{id}/status" body: "*" };
   322→  }
   323→}
```

```
1→syntax = "proto3";
     2→
     3→package shopstack.cart.v1;
     4→
     5→option go_package = "github.com/shopstack/platform/gen/go/cart/v1;cartv1";
     6→
     7→import "google/api/annotations.proto";
     8→import "google/protobuf/timestamp.proto";
     9→import "shopstack/common/v1/common.proto";
    10→
    11→message CartItem {
    12→  int64 id = 1;
    13→  int64 user_id = 2;
    14→  int64 product_id = 3;
    15→  int64 sku_id = 4;
    16→  string product_title = 5;
    17→  string spec_text = 6;
    18→  string image = 7;
    19→  int64 price_cents = 8;
    20→  int32 quantity = 9;
    21→  int32 stock = 10;
    22→  bool selected = 11;
    23→  bool available = 12;  // 库存不足 / 已下架
    24→  int64 subtotal_cents = 13;
    25→  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 14;
    26→  google.protobuf.Timestamp updated_at = 15;
    27→}
    28→
    29→message Cart {
    30→  int64 user_id = 1;
    31→  repeated CartItem items = 2;
    32→  int32 total_count = 3;          // 所有条目数量
    33→  int32 selected_count = 4;       // 选中条目数量
    34→  int64 selected_amount_cents = 5;// 选中商品总额
    35→  int64 selected_discount_cents = 6; // 选中优惠金额
    36→  int64 selected_payable_cents = 7;  // 选中应付金额
    37→}
    38→
    39→message GetCartRequest {
    40→  int64 user_id = 1;
    41→}
    42→
    43→message AddToCartRequest {
    44→  int64 user_id = 1;
    45→  int64 product_id = 2;
    46→  int64 sku_id = 3;
    47→  int32 quantity = 4;
    48→  bool selected = 5;
    49→}
    50→
    51→message UpdateCartItemRequest {
    52→  int64 user_id = 1;
    53→  int64 id = 2;
    54→  int32 quantity = 3;
    55→  bool selected = 4;
    56→}
    57→
    58→message RemoveCartItemRequest {
    59→  int64 user_id = 1;
    60→  int64 id = 2;
    61→}
    62→
    63→message ClearCartRequest {
    64→  int64 user_id = 1;
    65→}
    66→
    67→message SetAllSelectedRequest {
    68→  int64 user_id = 1;
    69→  bool selected = 2;
    70→}
    71→
    72→message GetCartCountRequest {
    73→  int64 user_id = 1;
    74→}
    75→
    76→message GetCartCountResponse {
    77→  int32 count = 1;
    78→}
    79→
    80→service CartService {
    81→  rpc GetCart(GetCartRequest) returns (Cart) {
    82→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/cart" };
    83→  }
    84→  rpc AddToCart(AddToCartRequest) returns (Cart) {
    85→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/cart/items" body: "*" };
    86→  }
    87→  rpc UpdateCartItem(UpdateCartItemRequest) returns (Cart) {
    88→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/cart/items/{id}" body: "*" };
    89→  }
    90→  rpc RemoveCartItem(RemoveCartItemRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
    91→    option (google.api.http) = { delete: "/api/v1/cart/items/{id}" };
    92→  }
    93→  rpc ClearCart(ClearCartRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
    94→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/cart/clear" body: "*" };
    95→  }
    96→  rpc SetAllSelected(SetAllSelectedRequest) returns (Cart) {
    97→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/cart/select-all" body: "*" };
    98→  }
    99→  rpc GetCount(GetCartCountRequest) returns (GetCartCountResponse) {
   100→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/cart/count" };
   101→  }
   102→}
```

```
1→syntax = "proto3";
     2→
     3→package shopstack.common.v1;
     4→
     5→option go_package = "github.com/shopstack/platform/gen/go/common/v1;commonv1";
     6→
     7→
     8→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     9→// 分页
    10→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    11→
    12→message PageRequest {
    13→  int32 page = 1;        // 页码，从 1 开始
    14→  int32 page_size = 2;   // 每页条数，默认 20，上限 100
    15→  string order_by = 3;   // 排序字段（白名单校验）
    16→  string order_dir = 4;  // asc | desc
    17→}
    18→
    19→message PageMeta {
    20→  int32 page = 1;
    21→  int32 page_size = 2;
    22→  int64 total = 3;
    23→  int32 total_pages = 4;
    24→}
    25→
    26→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→// 通用结构
    28→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→
    30→// 金额一律使用「分」为单位的 int64，避免浮点精度问题。
    31→message Money {
    32→  int64 amount_cents = 1;
    33→  string currency = 2;  // CNY
    34→}
    35→
    36→message Image {
    37→  string url = 1;
    38→  string alt = 2;
    39→}
    40→
    41→message Address {
    42→  int64 id = 1;
    43→  string receiver = 2;
    44→  string phone = 3;
    45→  string province = 4;
    46→  string city = 5;
    47→  string district = 6;
    48→  string detail = 7;
    49→  string postal_code = 8;
    50→  string tag = 9;  // 家 / 公司 / ���他
    51→  bool is_default = 10;
    52→}
    53→
    54→message ImageItem {
    55→  string url = 1;
    56→  string alt = 2;
    57→  int32 sort = 3;
    58→}
    59→
    60→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→// 枚举
    62→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→
    64→enum Gender {
    65→  GENDER_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
    66→  GENDER_MALE = 1;
    67→  GENDER_FEMALE = 2;
    68→  GENDER_UNKNOWN = 3;
    69→}
    70→
    71→enum UserStatus {
    72→  USER_STATUS_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
    73→  USER_STATUS_ACTIVE = 1;   // 正常
    74→  USER_STATUS_DISABLED = 2; // 禁用
    75→  USER_STATUS_FROZEN = 3;   // 冻结
    76→}
    77→
    78→enum ProductStatus {

    80→  PRODUCT_STATUS_DRAFT = 1;     // 草稿
    81→  PRODUCT_STATUS_ON_SHELF = 2;   // 已上架
    82→  PRODUCT_STATUS_OFF_SHELF = 3;  // 已下架
    83→}
    84→
    85→enum OrderStatus {
    86→  ORDER_STATUS_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
    87→  ORDER_STATUS_PENDING_PAYMENT = 1;  // 待付款
    88→  ORDER_STATUS_PAID = 2;             // 已付款/待发货
    89→  ORDER_STATUS_SHIPPED = 3;          // 待收货
    90→  ORDER_STATUS_COMPLETED = 4;        // 已完成
    91→  ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED = 5;        // 已取消
    92→  ORDER_STATUS_REFUNDING = 6;        // 退款中
    93→  ORDER_STATUS_REFUNDED = 7;         // 已退款
    94→  ORDER_STATUS_CLOSED = 8;           // 已关闭
    95→}
    96→
    97→enum PaymentStatus {
    98→  PAYMENT_STATUS_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
    99→  PAYMENT_STATUS_PENDING = 1;
   100→  PAYMENT_STATUS_SUCCESS = 2;
   101→  PAYMENT_STATUS_FAILED = 3;
   102→  PAYMENT_STATUS_CLOSED = 4;
   103→}
   104→
   105→enum PaymentChannel {

   109→  PAYMENT_CHANNEL_BALANCE = 3;  // 余额支付
   110→}
   111→
   112→enum ReviewStatus {

   114→  REVIEW_STATUS_PENDING = 1;
   115→  REVIEW_STATUS_APPROVED = 2;
   116→  REVIEW_STATUS_REJECTED = 3;
   117→}
   118→

   140→

   147→}
   148→
   149→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

   159→}
   160→
   161→message OperationResult {

   163→  string message = 2;
   164→}
   165→
   166→message Empty {}
```

```
1→syntax = "proto3";
     2→
     3→package shopstack.order.v1;
     4→
     5→option go_package = "github.com/shopstack/platform/gen/go/order/v1;orderv1";
     6→
     7→import "google/api/annotations.proto";
     8→import "google/protobuf/timestamp.proto";
     9→import "shopstack/common/v1/common.proto";
    10→
    11→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→// 实体
    13→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→message OrderItem {
    16→  int64 id = 1;
    17→  int64 order_id = 2;
    18→  int64 product_id = 3;
    19→  int64 sku_id = 4;
    20→  string product_title = 5;
    21→  string spec_text = 6;
    22→  string image = 7;
    23→  int64 price_cents = 8;
    24→  int32 quantity = 9;
    25→  int64 subtotal_cents = 10;
    26→  int64 refunded_cents = 11;
    27→  bool reviewed = 12;
    28→}
    29→
    30→message Order {
    31→  int64 id = 1;
    32→  string order_no = 2;
    33→  int64 user_id = 3;
    34→  string user_nickname = 4;
    35→  shopstack.common.v1.OrderStatus status = 5;
    36→  int64 total_amount_cents = 6;      // 商品总额
    37→  int64 discount_cents = 7;          // 优惠金额
    38→  int64 freight_cents = 8;           // 运费
    39→  int64 payable_cents = 9;           // 实付金额
    40→  int64 paid_cents = 10;             // 已付金额
    41→  int64 refunded_cents = 11;         // 已退金额
    42→  int32 item_count = 12;             // 商品件数
    43→  int32 total_quantity = 13;
    44→  shopstack.common.v1.Address address = 14;
    45→  string remark = 15;
    46→  string payment_method = 16;
    47→  google.protobuf.Timestamp paid_at = 17;
    48→  google.protobuf.Timestamp shipped_at = 18;
    49→  google.protobuf.Timestamp completed_at = 19;
    50→  google.protobuf.Timestamp cancelled_at = 20;
    51→  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 21;
    52→  google.protobuf.Timestamp updated_at = 22;
    53→  string admin_remark = 23;
    54→  int64 shipment_id = 24;
    55→}
    56→
    57→message OrderDetail {
    58→  Order order = 1;
    59→  repeated OrderItem items = 2;
    60→  repeated OrderStatusLog logs = 3;
    61→  int64 coupon_id = 4;
    62→  string coupon_name = 5;
    63→  int64 payment_id = 6;
    64→  int64 shipment_id = 7;
    65→  string tracking_no = 8;
    66→  string logistics_company = 9;
    67→  repeated string logistics_tracks = 10;
    68→  bool can_cancel = 11;
    69→  bool can_pay = 12;
    70→  bool can_confirm = 13;
    71→  bool can_refund = 14;
    72→  bool can_review = 15;
    73→}
    74→
    75→message OrderStatusLog {
    76→  int64 id = 1;
    77→  int64 order_id = 2;
    78→  int32 from_status = 3;
    79→  int32 to_status = 4;
    80→  string action = 5;
    81→  string operator = 6;
    82→  string remark = 7;
    83→  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 8;
    84→}
    85→
    86→message Refund {
    87→  int64 id = 1;
    88→  string refund_no = 2;
    89→  int64 order_id = 3;
    90→  string order_no = 4;
    91→  int64 user_id = 5;
    92→  int64 amount_cents = 6;
    93→  string reason = 7;
    94→  string description = 8;
    95→  repeated int64 item_ids = 9;
    96→  int32 status = 10;  // 0 待审核 1 已通过 2 已驳回 3 已退款
    97→  string admin_remark = 11;
    98→  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 12;
    99→  google.protobuf.Timestamp processed_at = 13;
   100→}
   101→
   102→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→// 请求 / 响应
   104→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   105→
   106→message CreateOrderItem {
   107→  int64 product_id = 1;
   108→  int64 sku_id = 2;
   109→  int32 quantity = 3;
   110→}
   111→
   112→message CreateOrderRequest {
   113→  int64 user_id = 1;
   114→  repeated CreateOrderItem items = 2;   // 直接下单
   115→  bool from_cart = 3;                   // 从购物车结算
   116→  repeated int64 cart_item_ids = 4;     // from_cart 时指定条目
   117→  int64 address_id = 5;
   118→  int64 coupon_id = 6;
   119→  string remark = 7;
   120→  string payment_method = 8; // wechat | alipay | balance
   121→  int64 points_used = 9;
   122→}
   123→
   124→message GetOrderRequest {
   125→  int64 id = 1;
   126→  int64 user_id = 2;
   127→}
   128→
   129→message ListOrdersRequest {
   130→  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 1;
   131→  int64 user_id = 2;
   132→  int32 status = 3;
   133→  string keyword = 4;   // 订单号
   134→  int64 start_time = 5; // unix 秒
   135→  int64 end_time = 6;
   136→}
   137→
   138→message ListOrdersResponse {
   139→  repeated Order orders = 1;
   140→  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
   141→  int64 pending_payment_count = 3;
   142→  int64 pending_shipment_count = 4;
   143→  int64 pending_receipt_count = 5;
   144→  int64 refunding_count = 6;
   145→}
   146→
   147→message CancelOrderRequest {
   148→  int64 order_id = 1;
   149→  int64 user_id = 2;
   150→  string reason = 3;
   151→}
   152→
   153→message ConfirmReceiptRequest {
   154→  int64 order_id = 1;
   155→  int64 user_id = 2;
   156→}
   157→
   158→message ApplyRefundRequest {
   159→  int64 order_id = 1;
   160→  int64 user_id = 2;
   161→  repeated int64 item_ids = 3;
   162→  int64 amount_cents = 4;
   163→  string reason = 5;
   164→  string description = 6;
   165→}
   166→
   167→message ListRefundsRequest {
   168→  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 1;
   169→  int32 status = 2;
   170→  int64 user_id = 3;
   171→  string keyword = 4;
   172→}
   173→
   174→message ListRefundsResponse {
   175→  repeated Refund refunds = 1;
   176→  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
   177→}
   178→
   179→message SetRefundStatusRequest {
   180→  int64 id = 1;
   181→  int32 status = 2;
   182→  string admin_remark = 3;
   183→}
   184→
   185→message AdminUpdateOrderRemarkRequest {
   186→  int64 id = 1;
   187→  string admin_remark = 2;
   188→}
   189→
   190→message ListOrderLogsRequest {
   191→  int64 order_id = 1;
   192→}
   193→
   194→message ListOrderLogsResponse {
   195→  repeated OrderStatusLog logs = 1;
   196→}
   197→
   198→message OrderCountRequest {
   199→  int64 user_id = 1;
   200→}
   201→
   202→message OrderCountResponse {
   203→  int64 pending_payment = 1;
   204→  int64 pending_shipment = 2;
   205→  int64 pending_receipt = 3;
   206→  int64 refunding = 4;
   207→}
   208→
   209→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   210→// 服务定义
   211→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   212→
   213→service OrderService {
   214→  rpc CreateOrder(CreateOrderRequest) returns (Order) {
   215→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/orders" body: "*" };
   216→  }
   217→  rpc GetOrder(GetOrderRequest) returns (OrderDetail) {
   218→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/orders/{id}" };
   219→  }
   220→  rpc ListOrders(ListOrdersRequest) returns (ListOrdersResponse) {
   221→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/orders" };
   222→  }
   223→  rpc CancelOrder(CancelOrderRequest) returns (Order) {
   224→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/orders/{order_id}/cancel" body: "*" };
   225→  }
   226→  rpc ConfirmReceipt(ConfirmReceiptRequest) returns (Order) {
   227→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/orders/{order_id}/confirm" body: "*" };
   228→  }
   229→  rpc ApplyRefund(ApplyRefundRequest) returns (Refund) {
   230→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/orders/{order_id}/refund" body: "*" };
   231→  }
   232→  rpc ListRefunds(ListRefundsRequest) returns (ListRefundsResponse) {
   233→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/admin/refunds" };
   234→  }
   235→  rpc SetRefundStatus(SetRefundStatusRequest) returns (Refund) {
   236→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/admin/refunds/{id}/status" body: "*" };
   237→  }
   238→  rpc UpdateOrderRemark(AdminUpdateOrderRemarkRequest) returns (Order) {
   239→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/admin/orders/{id}/remark" body: "*" };
   240→  }
   241→  rpc ListOrderLogs(ListOrderLogsRequest) returns (ListOrderLogsResponse) {
   242→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/orders/{order_id}/logs" };
   243→  }
```

```
1→syntax = "proto3";
     2→
     3→package shopstack.payment.v1;
     4→
     5→option go_package = "github.com/shopstack/platform/gen/go/payment/v1;paymentv1";
     6→
     7→import "google/api/annotations.proto";
     8→import "google/protobuf/timestamp.proto";
     9→import "shopstack/common/v1/common.proto";
    10→
    11→message Payment {
    12→  int64 id = 1;
    13→  string payment_no = 2;
    14→  int64 order_id = 3;
    15→  string order_no = 4;
    16→  int64 user_id = 5;
    17→  int64 amount_cents = 6;
    18→  int64 refunded_cents = 7;
    19→  shopstack.common.v1.PaymentStatus status = 8;
    20→  shopstack.common.v1.PaymentChannel channel = 9;
    21→  string transaction_id = 10;  // 第三方交易号
    22→  string prepay_id = 11;       // 唤起支付所需参数
    23→  map<string, string> pay_params = 12;
    24→  string fail_reason = 13;
    25→  google.protobuf.Timestamp paid_at = 14;
    26→  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 15;
    27→  google.protobuf.Timestamp updated_at = 16;
    28→}
    29→
    30→message PaymentRefund {
    31→  int64 id = 1;
    32→  string refund_no = 2;
    33→  int64 payment_id = 3;
    34→  int64 order_id = 4;
    35→  int64 amount_cents = 5;
    36→  int32 status = 6;
    37→  string reason = 7;
    38→  string transaction_id = 8;
    39→  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 9;
    40→}
    41→
    42→message CreatePaymentRequest {
    43→  int64 order_id = 1;
    44→  int64 user_id = 2;
    45→  int32 channel = 3;    // 1 微信 2 支付宝 3 余额
    46→  string return_url = 4;
    47→}
    48→
    49→message QueryPaymentRequest {
    50→  int64 payment_id = 1;
    51→  int64 order_id = 2;
    52→  int64 user_id = 3;
    53→}
    54→
    55→message PaymentCallbackRequest {
    56→  string payment_no = 1;
    57→  string transaction_id = 2;
    58→  string signature = 3;
    59→  string raw_body = 4;
    60→  int32 status = 5;
    61→}
    62→
    63→message CreateRefundRequest {
    64→  int64 payment_id = 1;
    65→  int64 order_id = 2;
    66→  int64 amount_cents = 3;
    67→  string reason = 4;
    68→}
    69→
    70→message ListPaymentsRequest {
    71→  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 1;
    72→  int32 status = 2;
    73→  int32 channel = 3;
    74→  string keyword = 4;
    75→  int64 user_id = 5;
    76→  int64 start_time = 6;
    77→  int64 end_time = 7;
    78→}
    79→
    80→message ListPaymentsResponse {
    81→  repeated Payment payments = 1;
    82→  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
    83→  int64 total_amount_cents = 3;
    84→  int64 total_refunded_cents = 4;
    85→}
    86→
    87→message PaymentSummaryRequest {
    88→  int32 days = 1;
    89→}
    90→
    91→message PaymentSummaryResponse {
    92→  int64 today_count = 1;
    93→  int64 today_amount_cents = 2;
    94→  int64 total_count = 3;
    95→  int64 total_amount_cents = 4;
    96→  int64 refunded_amount_cents = 5;
    97→  int64 success_rate = 6; // 百分比整数
    98→  repeated PaymentTrendPoint trend = 7;
    99→}
   100→
   101→message PaymentTrendPoint {
   102→  string date = 1;
   103→  int64 count = 2;
   104→  int64 amount_cents = 3;
   105→}
   106→
   107→service PaymentService {
   108→  rpc CreatePayment(CreatePaymentRequest) returns (Payment) {
   109→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/payments" body: "*" };
   110→  }
   111→  rpc QueryPayment(QueryPaymentRequest) returns (Payment) {
   112→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/payments/query" };
   113→  }
   114→  rpc Notify(PaymentCallbackRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   115→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/payments/notify" body: "*" };
   116→  }
   117→  rpc CreateRefund(CreateRefundRequest) returns (PaymentRefund) {
   118→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/payments/refund" body: "*" };
   119→  }
   120→  rpc ListPayments(ListPaymentsRequest) returns (ListPaymentsResponse) {
   121→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/admin/payments" };
   122→  }
   123→  rpc Summary(PaymentSummaryRequest) returns (PaymentSummaryResponse) {
   124→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/admin/payments/summary" };
   125→  }
   126→}
```

```
   124→  repeated AuditLog logs = 1;
   125→  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
   126→}
   127→
   128→service NotificationService {
   129→  rpc Send(SendNotificationRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   130→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/notifications/send" body: "*" };
   131→  }
   132→  rpc List(ListNotificationsRequest) returns (ListNotificationsResponse) {
   133→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/notifications" };
   134→  }
   135→  rpc MarkRead(MarkReadRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   136→    option (google.api.http) = { put: "/api/v1/notifications/{id}/read" body: "*" };
   137→  }
   138→  rpc MarkAllRead(MarkAllReadRequest) returns (shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult) {
   139→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/notifications/read-all" body: "*" };
   140→  }
   141→  rpc GetUnreadCount(UnreadCountRequest) returns (UnreadCountResponse) {
   142→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/notifications/unread-count" };
   143→  }
   144→  rpc SendSms(SendSmsRequest) returns (SendSmsResponse) {
   145→    option (google.api.http) = { post: "/api/v1/sms/send" body: "*" };
   146→  }
   147→  rpc ListSmsRecords(ListSmsRecordsRequest) returns (ListSmsRecordsResponse) {
   148→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/admin/sms-records" };
   149→  }
   150→  rpc ListAuditLogs(ListAuditLogsRequest) returns (ListAuditLogsResponse) {
   151→    option (google.api.http) = { get: "/api/v1/admin/audit-logs" };
   152→  }
   153→}
```

````
Mock fallback strategy: The API layer wraps each call; on network error (backend down), return mock data. The key requirement: "API 调用失败时，接口层返回内置 Mock 数据". So each api function has a try/catch that falls back to mock, and sets a global "demo mode" flag.

Design: `client.ts` exposes `request<T>(config, mockFactory)`. When request fails (network error or non-zero code?), we... Hmm. Requirement says "网络异常与后端未启动时要有兜底". Business errors (code != 0) should show error toast, not silently mock. But if backend returns error because not implemented... Let's do: network error / timeout / 5xx → mock fallback + set demo mode. HTTP 4xx / business error code != 0 → reject with message and toast.

Actually to be safe for demo purposes, if backend returns 404 (endpoint not found) → mock fallback. Let's define: fallback when `error.response` is undefined (network) or status >= 500 or status === 404.

Hmm, but business errors like "库存不足" should propagate. Those come with 200 + code!=0, or 400. So: 400/401/403/409 → real error. 404 → mock (route not implemented). 5xx/network → mock.

Auth: for demo, if backend is down, login should still work using mock tokens. Let's implement: auth login tries real API; on network failure returns mock auth response with fake token. Good — that makes the whole flow demoable.

Token storage: localStorage keys `ss_access_token`, `ss_refresh_token`.

401 handling: response interceptor catches 401 → if refresh token exists, call refresh once (with a shared promise), then replay original request once. Use config._retry flag.
````

```
=== server.go ===
// Package server 提供 gRPC 微服务统一的启动与拦截器能力。
package server

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"net"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"strings"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
	"google.golang.org/grpc"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/metadata"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/status"

	"github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/auth"
	shoplogger "github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/logger"
)

// Options 控制 gRPC 服务端行为。
type Options struct {
	ServiceName string
	Timeout     time.Duration
	// UnaryInterceptors 追加的自定义拦截器。
	UnaryInterceptors []grpc.UnaryServerInterceptor
	// StreamInterceptors 追加的自定义流拦截器。
	StreamInterceptors []grpc.StreamServerInterceptor
}

// NewGRPCServer 构造带标准拦截器链的 gRPC Server。
//
// 拦截器顺序：Recovery -> Trace -> Auth -> Logging -> 自定义
func NewGRPCServer(opts Options) *grpc.Server {
	timeout := opts.Timeout
	if timeout <= 0 {
		timeout = 30 * time.Second
	}

	chain := []grpc.UnaryServerInterceptor{
		recoveryInterceptor(),
		traceInterceptor(),
		authInterceptor(),
		loggingInterceptor(opts.ServiceName, timeout),
	}
	chain = append(chain, opts.UnaryInterceptors...)

	streamChain := []grpc.StreamServerInterceptor{
		streamRecoveryInterceptor(),
		streamTraceInterceptor(),
	}
	streamChain = append(streamChain, opts.StreamInterceptors...)

	return grpc.NewServer(
		grpc.ChainUnaryInterceptor(chain...),
		grpc.ChainStreamInterceptor(streamChain...),
		grpc.MaxRecvMsgSize(16*1024*1024),
		grpc.MaxSendMsgSize(16*1024*1024),
		grpc.ConnectionTimeout(10*time.Second),
		grpc.KeepaliveParams(keepaliveParams()),
	)
}

// Run 启动 gRPC 服务并等待退出信号，实现优雅停机。
func Run(ctx context.Context, srv *grpc.Server, addr, serviceName string) error {
	lis, err := net.Listen("tcp", addr)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("监听 %s 失败: %w", addr, err)
	}

	errCh := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		shoplogger.L().Info("gRPC 服务启动",
			zap.String("服务", serviceName), zap.String("地址", addr))
		if err := srv.Serve(lis); err != nil {
			errCh <- err
```

````
```ts
export async function apiGet<T>(url: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, fallback?: () => T): Promise<T>

Where fallback is invoked when the request fails in a "tolerable" way (network error, 404, 5xx). On fallback: set demoMode=true, return fallback().

401 refresh: implement in response interceptor:
```ts
client.interceptors.response.use(resp => resp, async error => {
  const original = error.config as RetryConfig
  if (error.response?.status === 401 && original && !original._retried && !original.url?.includes('/auth/')) {
     original._retried = true
     const ok = await refreshTokens()
     if (ok) return client(original)
     clearAuth(); redirect to /login
  }
  ...
})
```
refreshTokens uses a raw axios call (not the instance) to avoid recursion, with a shared promise.

Fallback detection in the error path: decide per error:
- no response (network/timeout) → fallback
- status 404 → fallback (route not implemented in this gateway version)
- status >= 500 → fallback
- else → throw with message

Also if the backend returns 200 with code != 0 → business error → throw (no fallback). But hmm: some business errors are legit (库存不足). Yes, throw.

Also for the envelope: `code !== 0` → error. But what if backend uses `code: 0` for success and also `success: true`? Just handle code.

Response normalization: convert camelCase → snake_case deeply, skipping map fields ('specs','pay_params') and any key whose parent key is in a skip set. Implementation:

```ts
const MAP_FIELDS = new Set(['specs', 'pay_params'])
function normalizeKeys(value, parentKey?) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(v => normalizeKeys(v, parentKey))
  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    const out = {}
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(value)) {
      const key = MAP_FIELDS.has(k) ? k : toSnake(k)
      out[key] = MAP_FIELDS.has(k) ? v : normalizeKeys(v, k)
    }
    return out
  }
  return value
}
```
Wait: if k is 'specs' we keep key and don't normalize its inner keys. But inner keys of specs are like "颜色" — normalizeKeys would leave them alone anyway (toSnake of CJK = same). Fine, but to be safe skip.

toSnake: 'accessToken' → 'access_token'; 'minPriceCents' → 'min_price_cents'; 'payParams' → 'pay_params'. Standard: replace /[A-Z]/g with '_' + lower.

But careful: 'ID' style? proto JSON names are like 'productId', 'skuId' → 'product_id', 'sku_id'. Good.

One risk: a legit user-data field with uppercase, e.g. spec "颜色" fine. Pay params keys like "appId" → would become "app_id" — but we skip pay_params. Good.
````

```
{
  "extends": "./tsconfig.json",
  "compilerOptions": {
    "lib": [
      // Target ES2022 to align with Vite.
      // <https://vite.dev/config/build-options.html#build-target>
      // Support for newer versions of language built-ins are
      // left for the users to include, because that would require:
      //   - either the project doesn't need to support older versions of browsers;
      //   - or the project has properly included the necessary polyfills.
      "ES2022",

      "DOM",
      "DOM.Iterable"

      // No `ScriptHost` because Vue 3 dropped support for IE
    ],

    // Set to empty to avoid accidental inclusion of unwanted types,
    // e.g. the Node.js types that would pollute the global scope.
    // It's the default value in TS 6+, too.
    "types": []
  }
}
{
  "compilerOptions": {
    // Most non-library projects don't need to emit declarations.
    // So we add this option by default to make the config more friendly to most users.
    "noEmit": true,

    // As long as you are using a build tool, we recommend you to author and ship in ES modules.
    // Even if you are targeting Node.js, because
    //  - `CommonJS` is too outdated
    //  - the ecosystem hasn't fully caught up with `Node16`/`NodeNext`
    // This recommendation includes environments like Vitest, Vite Config File, Vite SSR, etc.
    "module": "ESNext",

    // We expect users to use bundlers.
    // So here we enable some resolution features that are only available in bundlers.
    "moduleResolution": "bundler",
    "resolveJsonModule": true,
    "allowImportingTsExtensions": true,
    // Even files without `import` or `export` are treated as modules.
    // It helps to avoid mysterious errors such as `Cannot redeclare block-scoped variable 'name`.
    // https://www.totaltypescript.com/cannot-redeclare-block-scoped-variable#solution-3-your-module-isnt-a-module
    "moduleDetection": "force",

    // Required in Vue projects
    "jsx": "preserve",
    "jsxImportSource": "vue",

    // `"noImplicitThis": true` is part of `strict`
    // Added again here in case some users decide to disable `strict`.
    // This enables stricter inference for data properties on `this`.
    "noImplicitThis": true,
    // Strict mode is on by default in TS 6+
    "strict": true,

    // This option is part of the recommended tsconfig as of TS 5.9.
    // Commented out for now. It's hard to land in the current ecosystem.
    // Needs more consensus before moving forward.
    // "exactOptionalPropertyTypes": true,

    // <https://devblogs.microsoft.com/typescript/announcing-typescript-5-0/#verbatimmodulesyntax>
    // Any imports or exports without a type modifier are left around. This is important for `<script setup>`.
    // Anything that uses the type modifier is dropped entirely.
    "verbatimModuleSyntax": true,

    // A few notes:
    // - Vue 3 supports ES2016+
    // - For Vite, the actual compilation target is determined by the
    //   `build.target` option in the Vite config.
    //   So don't change the `target` field here. It has to be
    //   at least `ES2020` for dynamic `import()`s and `import.meta` to work correctly.
    // - If you are not using Vite, feel free to overwrite the `target` field.
    "target": "ESNext",
    // For spec compliance.
    // `true` by default if the `target` is `ES2020` or higher.
    // Explicitly set it to `true` here in case some users want to overwrite the `target`.
    "useDefineForClassFields": true,

    "forceConsistentCasingInFileNames": true,
    "libReplacement": false,
    // See <https://github.com/vuejs/vue-cli/pull/5688>
    "skipLibCheck": true,

}
```

````
Oops: in enums.ts, `pick` uses `map.__default__` which doesn't exist — TS error: Property '__default__' does not exist on type Record<string,string>. Actually Record<string, string> allows any string key access... `map.__default__` is allowed by TS for Record<string,string> index signature → returns string. It's fine type-wise (no error since index signature allows it). But `?? '—'` — the value could be undefined at runtime; TS thinks it's string so `??` is allowed? TS may complain "left operand of ?? can never be null" — that's only an error for `??` with non-nullable left? TS gives error "Right operand of ?? is unreachable because the left operand is never nullish" only in some cases. Actually TS does NOT error on that for `??` with string type... Hmm, TS does report error 2869? Let me avoid risk: simplify `pick` to avoid `__default__`.

Let me rewrite pick:

```ts
function pick(map: Record<string, string>, value?: string | number | null): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') return '—'
  const key = String(value)
  return map[key] ?? key
}
```
And for unspecified statuses, the maps have 'GENDER_UNSPECIFIED' etc. But value '' returns '—'. Fine. Actually for GENDER_UNSPECIFIED the map has '未设置'. Good.
````

````
```ts
const MAP_KEYS = new Set(['specs', 'pay_params'])
function toSnake(key: string): string {
  return key.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2').replace(/([A-Z]+)([A-Z][a-z])/g, '$1_$2').toLowerCase()
}
function normalize(value: unknown, parentKey?: string): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(v => normalize(v, parentKey))
  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    const out: Record<string, unknown> = {}
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)) {
      if (MAP_KEYS.has(k)) { out[k] = v; continue }
      out[toSnake(k)] = normalize(v, k)
    }
    return out
  }
  return value
}
```

```ts
async function request<T>(config: AxiosRequestConfig, opts: { fallback?: () => T; silent?: boolean } = {}): Promise<T> {
  try {
    const resp = await client.request(config)
    return resp.data as T
  } catch (err) {
    const apiErr = toApiError(err)
    if (opts.fallback && isFallbackError(apiErr)) {
      useAppStore().enterDemoMode(apiErr.message)
      return opts.fallback()
    }
    if (!opts.silent) useAppStore().toast(apiErr.message, 'error')
    throw apiErr
  }
}
```

isFallbackError: apiErr.status === 0 (network) or 404 or >= 500.

Now the refresh implementation:

```ts
let refreshPromise: Promise<boolean> | null = null
async function refreshTokens(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!refreshPromise) {
    refreshPromise = doRefresh().finally(() => { refreshPromise = null })
  }
  return refreshPromise
}
async function doRefresh(): Promise<boolean> {
  const rt = getRefreshToken()
  if (!rt) return false
  try {
    const resp = await plainClient.post('/api/v1/auth/refresh', { refresh_token: rt })
    const body = resp.data
    if (body && body.code === 0 && body.data) {
      setAccessToken(body.data.access_token)
      if (body.data.refresh_token) setRefreshToken(body.data.refresh_token)
      return true
    }
    return false
  } catch { return false }
}
```
where plainClient = axios.create({baseURL, timeout}) without interceptors.

Then in the error interceptor:
```ts
if (status === 401 && !config._retried && !config._skipAuthRefresh) {
  config._retried = true
  const ok = await refreshTokens()
  if (ok) return client.request(config)
  clearAuthStorage()
}
```

I'll keep demo flag in app store but guard with try/catch: if pinia isn't ready, skip.

Actually simplest robust approach: keep a module-level `reactive` demo state in `src/stores/app.ts`? No—app store is a Pinia store; but I can also export a plain reactive object from a separate module `src/utils/demo.ts`... Overkill. I'll just call useAppStore() inside try/catch. Since main.ts does `app.use(pinia)` before mount, and requests only fire from components after mount, it's fine.
````

````
Let me write mock/index.ts:

```ts
import { ... } from './data'

// 分页工具
function paginate<T>(list: T[], page = 1, pageSize = 20) {
  const total = list.length
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize))
  const start = (page - 1) * pageSize
  return { list: list.slice(start, start + pageSize), meta: { page, page_size: pageSize, total, total_pages: totalPages } }
}
```
````

````
Now the composable `src/composables/useAddToCart.ts`:

```ts
import { getProduct } from '@/api/product'
export function useAddToCart() {
  const cart = useCartStore()
  const app = useAppStore()
  const loading = ref(false)
  async function add(productId: number, quantity = 1) {
    loading.value = true
    try {
      const detail = await getProduct(productId)
      const sku = detail.skus.find(s => s.enabled && s.stock > 0)
      if (!sku) { app.error('该商品暂时缺货'); return }
      await cart.addToCart({product_id: productId, sku_id: sku.id, quantity})
      app.success('已加入购物车')
    } catch { /* 错误提示已处理 */ }
    finally { loading.value = false }
  }
  return { add, loading }
}
```
Note getProduct fallback returns mock detail, so this works in demo mode too.
````

````
Now ProductDetailView — the most complex one.

Features:
- carousel of images
- title/subtitle
- price: current (selected sku price) + original
- sales, stock
- SKU spec selection: derive spec keys; for each key, options; selecting a key filters available skus; selected sku = matching sku
- attributes table

Implementation of spec selection:
```ts
const specKeys = computed(() => {
  const keys: string[] = []
  detail.value?.skus.forEach(sku => Object.keys(sku.specs).forEach(k => { if (!keys.includes(k)) keys.push(k) }))
  return keys
})
const selectedSpecs = reactive<Record<string,string>>({})
const selectedSku = computed(() => {
  const skus = detail.value?.skus ?? []
  return skus.find(sku => specKeys.value.every(k => (sku.specs[k] ?? '') === (selectedSpecs[k] ?? ''))) ?? null
})
```
On load, initialize selectedSpecs with the first sku's specs.
````

````
- If refresh fails:

  - If no fallback → clear the auth state + dispatch.

Implementation: in the interceptor, on refresh failure, set a marker `_authFailed` on the config and reject. In request()'s catch:
````

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

````
Everything passes with vue-router 4 after a clean install.

Final: run the build one more time (after the .DS_Store cleanup, nothing changed in source). Let me do a final build to confirm and then stop the dev server? The dev server should probably be left stopped to not interfere. Actually leaving it running is fine but it holds a port; the parent might want to run it. I'll stop it to leave a clean state... Actually the task says "npm run dev 能启动且首页正常渲染" — verified. I'll kill it to free the port.

Let me do the final build and kill the dev server.

Also one last consideration about the demo: the gateway on :8080 currently returns 401 with a different envelope shape (`{"code":"unauthorized"}`) — my client treats non-zero code as an error → falls back to mock → demo mode. Good.

Let me also double check `src/api/client.ts` handles a non-0 code body without `data` — `if (body && 'code' in body && 'data' in body)` — the gateway's body has no `data` field → falls to the else branch → response.data = normalizeKeys(body) → returns the whole error body as data! That would return `{"code":"unauthorized","message":"..."}` as the data for a 200 response. Hmm — for the 401 case the response status is 401 so it goes to the error path anyway. But if the gateway returned HTTP 200 with `{"code":"unauthorized"}`, my client would return that object as data instead of throwing. Let me tighten the envelope detection: treat a body with a `code` property as an envelope (require 'code' in body only, and data optional). Then `body.code !== 0` → throw ApiError with the message. That's more correct.

But careful: some legit data payloads might contain a `code` field... e.g. promo coupon `code` (the coupon's code string like 'NEW20')! If a response data contains a top-level 'code' key... e.g. `GET /api/v1/coupons` returns `{coupons: [...], meta: {...}}` — no top-level 'code'. The Coupon.message has `code` but nested. The envelope check happens on the top-level body only. A coupon list response has no top-level 'code' ✓.

Risk: an endpoint returning a raw proto message that HAS a top-level `code` field → e.g. LoginRequest has 'code'? No. `WeChatLoginRequest.code` — that's a request. `Coupon.code` is nested in an array. `PaymentCallbackRequest.code`? no. `Review`? no. So the only top-level 'code' responses would be from the envelope. 

I'll change the detection to `'code' in body` and treat `data` as optional. Let me implement:

```ts
if (body && typeof body === 'object' && 'code' in body) {
  lastTraceId = (body as {trace_id?: string}).trace_id ?? ''
  if (body.code !== 0) throw new ApiError(body.message || '请求失败', body.code, response.status, lastTraceId)
  response.data = normalizeKeys((body as {data?: unknown}).data)
  return response
}
```
This handles envelopes with data present or absent. And a raw proto response without a top-level 'code' is unaffected.

Hmm, but one more: `normalizeKeys(body.data)` where data is undefined → returns undefined ✓.

Let me make this change, rebuild, and re-verify quickly.
````

**实现页面**（`shopstack/apps/web-vue/`，Vue3 + TS strict + Pinia + Vue Router 4 + Axios，无重型 UI 库，手写令牌化样式）：首页、分类页、商品详情、购物车、结算页、收银台、订单列表/详情、个人中心、地址管理、我的收藏、我的优惠券、消息通知、登录/注册、搜索页、404。组件含 ProductCard / PriceText / QuantityStepper / AppHeader / AppFooter / EmptyState / LoadingState / Skeleton / Pagination / AddressPicker，外加 Carousel、AddressFormModal、RefundDialog、StarRating、ToastHost、DemoBanner。

**Build**：`npm install && npm run build` 全新安装后通过，产出 `dist/`（552K）。

**Mock 兜底**：接口层 `src/api/client.ts` 拆解 `{code,message,data,trace_id}` 信封、注入 token、401 静默刷新并只重放一次；网络不通/404/5xx/401 时回退 `src/mock/`（字段与 proto 一致），顶部显示「演示数据模式」提示条。

**偏离**：① dev 用 Vite 代理 `/api`→`localhost:8080`（免跨域，可用 `VITE_GATEWAY_URL` 覆盖），未直接写死绝对 baseURL；② 分页参数按 grpc-gateway 规则用 `page.page`/`page.page_size`；③ 响应键名做了 camelCase→snake_case 归一化，兼容两种后端序列化；④ 当前 8080 上的网关对所有请求返回 401（与任务描述的契约不符），前端因此整体处于演示模式。

## 👤 用户 · 2026-10-20 12:24:35

【扩量任务 · Vue3 用户端前台】新增 14 个页面，把用户端从 16 页扩到 30 页。

## 你的唯一工作目录
`/<WORKSPACE>/shopstack/apps/web-vue`
**只允许修改这个目录下的文件**，不要碰 `backend/`、`proto/`、`apps/` 下其他三个前端目录。

## 目标
在现有 16 个 view 基础上新增 14 个**功能完整、可交互、非占位**的页面，总数达到 30 页。

## 硬性接口契约（务必严格遵守）
- 网关 base：`http://localhost:8080`（项目默认端口）。**你上一轮发现 8080 上跑的是一个返回 401 的无关网关**——那不是本项目。本项目网关的正确行为是：`/api/v1/auth/*` 与 `/api/v1/admin/auth/login` 免鉴权，其余路径校验 Bearer token。本机项目网关实测在 **8090** 端口（因为 8080 被别的进程占着）。联调时用 `VITE_GATEWAY_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8090` 覆盖，Vite 代理默认值仍保持 8080 不变。
- 统一响应信封：`{ "code": 0, "message": "ok", "data": {...}, "trace_id": "..." }`，**`code === 0` 才是成功**。
- JSON 字段名**一律 snake_case**，与 proto 完全一致（如 `min_price_cents`、`order_no`、`created_at`）。
- 64 位整数（`id` / `*_cents` / `*_id`）在 JSON 里是**数字**，不是字符串。
- 金额单位是**分**，展示要除以 100——复用你已有的 `PriceText` / 金额工具。
- 分页 query 用**点号嵌套形式**：`?page.page=1&page.page_size=20`。
- 鉴权头：`Authorization: Bearer <token>`。

## 要新增的 14 个页面

| 路由 | 页面名 | 接口 |
|---|---|---|
| `/points` | 积分中心 | `GET /api/v1/users/points-logs?page.page=1&page.page_size=20` |
| `/invoices` | 发票管理 | `GET /api/v1/users/invoices?page.page=1&page.page_size=20` / `POST /api/v1/users/invoices` |
| `/logistics` | 物流跟踪 | `GET /api/v1/logistics/tracks?order_id=` / `GET /api/v1/logistics/order/{order_id}` |
| `/coupon-center` | 领券中心 | `GET /api/v1/coupons?page.page=1&page.page_size=20` / `POST /api/v1/coupons/{coupon_id}/receive` |
| `/reviews` | 我的评价 | `GET /api/v1/reviews?product_id=` / `POST /api/v1/reviews` |
| `/refund` | 售后申请 | `GET /api/v1/orders?page.page=1&page.page_size=20` / `POST /api/v1/orders/{order_id}/refund` |
| `/pay-result` | 支付结果 | `GET /api/v1/payments/query?order_id=` |
| `/flash-sale` | 限时秒杀 | `GET /api/v1/promotions` + `GET /api/v1/products` |
| `/group-buy` | 拼团专区 | `GET /api/v1/promotions` + `GET /api/v1/products` |
| `/member` | 会员中心 | `GET /api/v1/users/me` + `GET /api/v1/users/points-logs` + `GET /api/v1/users/coupons` |
| `/history` | 浏览足迹 | 本地存储（localStorage） |
| `/compare` | 商品对比 | 本地 + `GET /api/v1/products/{id}` |
| `/categories` | 全部分类 | `GET /api/v1/categories` |
| `/order-trace` | 订单轨迹 | `GET /api/v1/orders/{id}/logs` |

> 注意：**现有 `/coupons` 页是「我的优惠券」**（走 `GET /api/v1/users/coupons`），新增的「领券中心」走 `GET /api/v1/coupons`（平台可领券），两者不同，路由不要撞。

### 各接口关键字段

**积分中心** `PointsLog`：`id / change / type / remark / order_no / created_at`。响应 `data.logs` + `data.meta` + `data.current_points`（当前积分）。`type` 做中文映射（earn=获得、consume=消费、expire=过期、refund=退回），正数绿、负数红。

**发票管理** `Invoice`：`id / order_no / title(抬头) / tax_no(税号) / type(personal|company) / email / amount_cents / status(0待开票 1已开票 2已驳回) / invoice_url / remark / created_at`。`POST` body：`{ "order_no", "title", "tax_no", "type", "email", "amount_cents", "remark" }`（以 `proto/shopstack/user/v1/user.proto` 为准，只读不改）。页面要有「申请开票」表单（个人/企业抬头切换，企业需填税号）+ 状态标签 + 已开票的可下载链接。

**物流跟踪**：`GET /api/v1/logistics/tracks?order_id=<int64>` 返回轨迹数组；`GET /api/v1/logistics/order/{order_id}` 返回发货单（承运商/运单号/状态）。页面做成「输入订单号查询」+ 时间轴展示。物流服务刚实现完，若接口暂时 503，显示友好空态 + 重试，不能白屏。

**领券中心**：`GET /api/v1/coupons` 返回可领券（`id / name / type(满减|折扣|免运费) / amount_cents / discount_percent / min_amount_cents / total_quantity / received_quantity / expire_at / description`）。「立即领取」调 `POST /api/v1/coupons/{coupon_id}/receive`（body `{}`），成功后按钮变「已领取」并禁用，防重复提交。

**我的评价**：`POST /api/v1/reviews` body：`{ "product_id", "sku_id", "rating"(1-5), "content", "images": [] }`（以 proto 为准）。支持从已购订单里选商品 → 打星 → 写文字 → 选图（无 OSS 时用 Mock 占位图 URL）。列表用 `GET /api/v1/reviews?product_id=` 展示。

**售后申请**：`POST /api/v1/orders/{order_id}/refund` body：`{ "reason", "amount_cents", "description" }`（以 proto 为准）。上半部分可申请退款的订单列表（已付款/已完成），下半部分退款表单（选退款原因、填金额、补充说明）。注意你已有一个 `RefundDialog` 组件，可以复用它的表单逻辑。

**支付结果**：`GET /api/v1/payments/query?order_id=<int64>` 返回支付单（`payment_no / status(PAYMENT_STATUS_SUCCESS 等) / paid_cents / paid_at`）。成功态给「查看订单」按钮跳订单详情，失败态给「重新支付」。

**限时秒杀 / 拼团专区**：`GET /api/v1/promotions` 返回活动列表（`id / name / type(秒杀|拼团|满减|折扣) / start_at / end_at / description / rules`），配 `GET /api/v1/products` 里的商品做卡片。倒计时按 `end_at` 实时计算。**注意这俩页共用 promotions 接口，但呈现方式要明显不同**：秒杀页是倒计时 + 抢购按钮（跳结算页），拼团页是成团人数 + 参团按钮。

**会员中心** `User`：`id / username / nickname / avatar / phone / points / level` 等（以 proto 为准）。聚合展示会员等级、当前积分、累计获得积分、我的优惠券数量，并放各子页入口。

**浏览足迹**：纯本地，记录最近浏览商品（名称/图片/价格），localStorage 存，单条删除 + 清空 + 点击跳详情。**商品详情页浏览时要写入足迹**（你已有 `ProductDetailView`，接一下）。

**商品对比**：本地存选中的 SKU（最多 4 个），对比表展示图片/名称/价格/规格/库存/销量，支持移除某项、加入对比、清空。

**全部分类**：`GET /api/v1/categories` 返回分类树。做成左侧树形导航 + 右侧该分类下的商品列表（`GET /api/v1/products?category_id=`）。

**订单轨迹**：`GET /api/v1/orders/{id}/logs` 返回订单状态流转日志（`action / remark / created_at`）。做成时间轴，支持输入订单号查询。

## 实施要求
1. **沿用现有范式**：Vue3 + TS strict + Pinia + Vue Router 4 + Axios，你已有的 `src/api/client.ts`（信封拆解 / token 注入 / 401 刷新 / mock 兜底）、`src/mock/`、`src/components/`（ProductCard / PriceText / QuantityStepper / EmptyState / LoadingState / Skeleton / Pagination / AddressPicker / Carousel / RefundDialog / StarRating / ToastHost / DemoBanner 等）**全部复用**，不要重写。
2. **Mock 兜底必须做**：14 个新页面**每个都要有对应 Mock 数据**，后端不通时回退 `src/mock/`，顶部显示「演示数据模式」。数据字段必须与 proto 一致（snake_case）。
3. **路由注册**：`src/router` 注册 14 条路由。入口要**真实可达**——个人中心/用户中心加「积分中心 / 发票管理 / 物流跟踪 / 领券中心 / 我的评价 / 售后申请 / 订单轨迹」入口，导航栏加「全部分类 / 限时秒杀 / 拼团专区」，商品详情页加「加入对比 / 浏览足迹」入口。不要只建文件没人点得到。
4. **禁止占位**：所有按钮都要有真实行为，不允许 TODO / 空函数 / `console.log` 占位。
5. **不改其它目录**。
6. **验证**：headless Chromium（沿用你上一轮的 Playwright 冒烟方式）跑通全部 30 条路由 + 新增页面的交互流，确认 0 console 错误、0 未捕获 Promise。

## 交付时汇报
列出：14 个新页面的路由 → 文件路径 → 用的接口；入口埋点位置；build 与冒烟结果；Mock 覆盖情况；以及任何你发现的后端契约问题（不要自己改 proto）。

```
}

message ToggleFavoriteResponse {
  bool favorited = 1;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 积分明细与发票
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message PointsLog {
  int64 id = 1;
  int64 user_id = 2;
  string title = 3;         // 变动说明
  int32 change = 4;         // 正数为增加，负数为减少
  int32 balance = 5;        // 变动后余额
  string biz_type = 6;      // register / order / exchange / admin
  string biz_id = 7;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 8;
}

message ListPointsLogsRequest {
  int64 user_id = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 2;
}

message ListPointsLogsResponse {
  repeated PointsLog logs = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
  int32 current_points = 3;
}

message Invoice {
  int64 id = 1;
  int64 user_id = 2;
  string order_no = 3;
  string title = 4;         // 发票抬头
  string tax_no = 5;        // 纳税人识别号
  string type = 6;          // personal / company
  string email = 7;
  int64 amount_cents = 8;
  int32 status = 9;         // 0 待开票 1 已开票 2 已驳回
  string invoice_url = 10;
  string remark = 11;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 12;
}

message ListInvoicesRequest {
  int64 user_id = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 2;
  int32 status = 3;
}

message SetInvoiceStatusRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
  int32 status = 2;        // 0 待开票 1 已开票 2 已驳回
  string invoice_url = 3;  // 开票后回填的电子发票地址
  string remark = 4;       // 驳回原因
}

message ListInvoicesResponse {
  repeated Invoice invoices = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
}

message CreateInvoiceRequest {
  int64 user_id = 1;
  string order_no = 2;
  string title = 3;
  string tax_no = 4;
  string type = 5;
  string email = 6;
  int64 amount_cents = 7;
  string remark = 8;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 后台管理
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

message AdminLoginRequest {
=== USER MSG ===
message User {
  int64 id = 1;
  string username = 2;
  string nickname = 3;
  string avatar = 4;
  string phone = 5;
  string email = 6;
  shopstack.common.v1.Gender gender = 7;
  string birthday = 8;
  int32 points = 9;   // 积分
  int64 balance_cents = 10; // 余额（分）
  shopstack.common.v1.UserStatus status = 11;
  string wechat_openid = 12;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 13;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp last_login_at = 14;
}

message Admin {
  int64 id = 1;
  string username = 2;
  string real_name = 3;
  string avatar = 4;
  string email = 5;
  string phone = 6;
  repeated int64 role_ids = 7;
  repeated string role_names = 8;
```

```
syntax = "proto3";

package shopstack.marketing.v1;

option go_package = "github.com/shopstack/platform/gen/go/marketing/v1;marketingv1";

import "google/api/annotations.proto";
import "google/protobuf/timestamp.proto";
import "shopstack/common/v1/common.proto";

message Coupon {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  string code = 3;
  shopstack.common.v1.CouponType type = 4;
  int64 face_value_cents = 5;  // 满减金额
  int32 percent = 6;           // 折扣百分比，如 85 表示 8.5 折
  int64 min_amount_cents = 7;  // 使用门槛
  int64 max_discount_cents = 8;// 最大优惠金额
  int64 total_quantity = 9;    // 发行总量
  int64 received_quantity = 10;
  int64 used_quantity = 11;
  int64 per_user_limit = 12;
  int64 start_time = 13;
  int64 end_time = 14;
  int64 category_id = 15;      // 限定分类，0 = 全场
  bool enabled = 16;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 17;
}

message UserCoupon {
  int64 id = 1;
  int64 user_id = 2;
  int64 coupon_id = 3;
  Coupon coupon = 4;
  shopstack.common.v1.UserCouponStatus status = 5;
  int64 order_id = 6;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp received_at = 7;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp used_at = 8;
}

message Promotion {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  string description = 3;
  int32 type = 4;       // 1 满减 2 折扣 3 秒杀 4 拼团
  int32 status = 5;     // 0 未开始 1 进行中 2 已结束 3 已下线
  int64 start_time = 6;
  int64 end_time = 7;
  int64 min_amount_cents = 8;
  int64 discount_cents = 9;
  int32 percent = 10;
  int64 product_id = 11;
  int32 limit_per_user = 12;
  int64 sold_count = 13;
  string banner_image = 14;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 15;
}

message ListCouponsRequest {
  shopstack.common.v1.PageRequest page = 1;
  string keyword = 2;
  int32 type = 3;
  bool enabled = 4;
  bool available = 5; // 仅当前可领取
}

message ListCouponsResponse {
  repeated Coupon coupons = 1;
  shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta meta = 2;
}

message SaveCouponRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
  string name = 2;
  string code = 3;
  int32 type = 4;
  int64 face_value_cents = 5;
  int32 percent = 6;
  int64 min_amount_cents = 7;
```

## 🤖 MiniMax-M3.1-Flash · 2026-10-20 12:25:15

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

```
Key contract findings:
- `users/coupons` **requires** `user_id` (without it → code 1001 "用户 ID 不能为空"). addresses/favorites/points-logs/invoices work without user_id (but points-logs/invoices presumably also default to the token user; they worked without user_id too — yes, points-logs without user_id worked earlier).
- So only `users/coupons` needs user_id. My existing api/marketing.listUserCoupons must add user_id (from the auth store / profile).

Let me now plan the implementation.

### New API modules & endpoints
Add to existing api files:
- `api/user.ts`: listPointsLogs(params), listInvoices(params), createInvoice(payload)
- `api/logistics.ts`: listTracks(order_id)
- `api/marketing.ts`: listCoupons (exists, but needs `available`?), receiveCoupon(coupon_id)
- `api/product.ts`: createReview exists; listReviews exists (add user_id?)
- `api/order.ts`: applyRefund exists (body should be {reason, amount_cents, description} per proto ApplyRefundRequest: order_id, user_id, item_ids, amount_cents, reason, description — the http rule is post /api/v1/orders/{order_id}/refund body:"*" so order_id is in the path AND the body may contain it. Fine.)
- `api/payment.ts`: queryPayment exists ✓

### New pages (14)
1. `/points` PointsView — GET /api/v1/users/points-logs
2. `/invoices` InvoicesView — GET/POST /api/v1/users/invoices
3. `/logistics` LogisticsView — GET /api/v1/logistics/tracks?order_id= + GET /api/v1/logistics/order/{id}
4. `/coupon-center` CouponCenterView — GET /api/v1/coupons + POST /api/v1/coupons/{id}/receive
5. `/reviews` MyReviewsView — GET /api/v1/reviews + POST /api/v1/reviews
6. `/refund` RefundView — GET /api/v1/orders + POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/refund
7. `/pay-result` PayResultView — GET /api/v1/payments/query?order_id=
8. `/flash-sale` FlashSaleView — GET /api/v1/promotions + products
9. `/group-buy` GroupBuyView — GET /api/v1/promotions + products
10. `/member` MemberView — GET /api/v1/users/me + points-logs + users/coupons
11. `/history` HistoryView — localStorage
12. `/compare` CompareView — localStorage + GET /api/v1/products/{id}
13. `/categories` CategoriesView — GET /api/v1/categories + GET /api/v1/products?category_id=
14. `/order-trace` OrderTraceView — GET /api/v1/orders/{id}/logs

### Local stores (new)
- `src/stores/history.ts` — 浏览足迹 (localStorage), max 20 items
```
